# Changes and visual adjudications

Each row is one recorded place, not a token count. C/H columns count substantive or diplomatic differences from each witness; a row can count against both. Resolution of a source doubt is included when recorded as a reading decision. New uncertainty alone is listed in the doubts file. Macro-only differences and H editorial annotations are excluded. Pixel boxes refer to the supplied original scans.

## Consequential corrections

- Scan 25, opening diagram / lower right-hand expression: The written expression is Y_0 cap E, although the product Y_0 times I appears in the lower compatibility display. No correction is imported from that later display.
- Scan 25, line 6 / small cancelled identification below dotted E cap T: The cancelled endpoint expression is left unclosed as written; the missing right brace is not completed.
- Scan 25, line 15 / T_0-T_0: The map interpretation is suggested by the prose, but does not settle the written connector.
- Scan 25, line 23 / overwritten verb before sur T_E: The exact grammar of the final compatibility condition remains uncertain at this overwritten verb.
- Scan 25, line 23 / T_E: The proposed reading does not introduce closure notation absent from the ink.
- Scan 26, line 6 / arrow in the descriptive tuple: The final item of the tuple is a map from dot X to Y.
- Scan 26, line 8 / structure cône: This records the author’s noun-form terminology structure cône; it is not a change to a mathematical claim.
- Scan 26, line 9 / tilde on the neighborhood symbol in the system: The marked neighborhood germ over tilde X_0 is distinguished from the other neighborhood germs.
- Scan 26, lines 17–18 / vertical identity annotation: The annotation identifies dot X_0 with the intersection tilde X_0 cap dot X, retaining the manuscript’s vertical geometry.
- Scan 26, lines 17–18 / location and index of inserted dans dot X: The inserted ambient space belongs to the inherited cylindrical structure in dot X.
- Scan 26, line 24 / first product relation: The first product relation is an isomorphism sign, as in the second product, rather than literal equality.
- Scan 27, line 1 / detached stroke after the second I: Potentially changes the interval label; both identity and attachment remain unresolved.
- Scan 27, line 3 / finie: The finiteness qualifier is canceled here; I fini remains later in the sentence.
- Scan 27, diagonal margin / isomorphism sign before B_J: Preserves direction of the indicated map.
- Scan 27, diagonal margin / small annotation beneath the arrow: The construction asserted to identify the dotted intersection with B_J remains unresolved.
- Scan 27, line 13 / germ statement: Restores the assertion that the relation passes to germs.
- Scan 27, lower display / T_J in first row and its definition: Avoids introducing a closure or barred object absent from the source.
- Scan 27, lower display / vertical inclusions: Restores the parallel vertical inclusion on the product side.
- Scan 27, lower display / final definition: Preserves the apparent author slip; an intersection is mathematically expected but is not the visible sign.
- Scan 28, top continuation / word cut at right paper edge: The physical truncation remains visible in the transcription.
- Scan 28, c) / opening and placement of I-index: Restores the attachment of the interval label to the ordered set being described.
- Scan 28, c) / inserted order word: Retains the literal minimaux; an apparent mathematical mismatch is recorded in the notes.
- Scan 28, c) / j in inequality and final double subscript: The possibly problematic j ≥ s_k is retained exactly, without a mathematical emendation.
- Scan 29, upper-right corner drawing / five hatches and contour placement: Preserves the actual number and disposition of drawn strokes; no mathematical interpretation of the drawing is supplied.
- Scan 29, first diagram / the two vertical order signs: Distinguishes the order relations j′ ≥ k′ and j ≥ k, drawn vertically, from directed morphisms.
- Scan 29, set calculation / lower-case j in first line: Retains the manuscript’s local j notation and its literal k∈j, without repairing the apparent set-theoretic slip.
- Scan 29, reference after l’hyp. / number and closing parenthesis: The hypothesis reference remains uncertain, with its legible leading digit and closing parenthesis preserved.
- Scan 29, bottom pair expression / headless connector: The manuscript’s headless stroke is retained rather than supplied with a direction.
- Scan 30, left margin beside (14) / full intersection-union formula: Preserves the written lower-bound condition and records a possible grouping sign without normalizing it.
- Scan 30, line 15 / une partie de I zero: The page literally names I_0 a second time; a probable authorial slip is retained.
- Scan 31, map connector / retained graphical doubt: Direction is not supplied where the ink leaves dash/arrow unresolved.
- Scan 31, upper equality / first E has no overbar: Distinguishes plain E on the left from barred E inside the inverse image.
- Scan 31, upper equality / first index and comma: Retains the first k prime and the literal comma separator.
- Scan 31, corollary / minorant h: Preserves the manuscript’s h/k mismatch rather than repairing the variable.
- Scan 31, corollary / detached graphical mark before inequality: Accounts for visible ink without forcing it into a mathematical operation.
- Scan 32, cancelled (18) / absent relation in second column: Preserves stacking while removing an unwritten identity sign; H has only a summarized mathematical identity.
- Scan 32, cancelled map / final visible parenthesis: Retains the visible unmatched closing grouping in the local transcription.
- Scan 33, third condition / induced pair without stars: The written induced pair is unstarred even though nearby boundary pairs are starred; this apparent mismatch is preserved.
- Scan 36, line 2 / coniques: The source has singular donnée followed by plural coniques; the agreement is not repaired.
- Scan 36, line 2 / opening tuple right edge: The visible opening tuple remains unclosed at the paper edge.
- Scan 36, line 9 / detached mark above alpha: The extra mark is retained without inventing a mathematical meaning for it.
- Scan 36, display (22) / left annotation without brace: The annotation is placed below the family without supplying a brace or bringing the standalone X-star under one.
- Scan 36, display (23) / supplied outer opening parenthesis: The source display remains parenthetically unbalanced.
- Scan 36, line 16 / infinity sign after colon: The standalone infinity-shaped sign is retained without assigning it a role in the definition.
- Scan 37, line 2 / non sauvagerie: The stated hypothesis concerns non-wildness, not the absence of singularity.
- Scan 37, line 6 / second union-bound row: Both written lower-bound rows are retained; their intended relation or revision order is not inferred.
- Scan 37, line 6 / alors il doit =: The verbal shorthand =, probably même, is retained without expansion.
- Scan 37, line 12 / penultimate filtration index: Resolves C’s marked filtration index as n-1, without changing the filtration mathematics.
- Scan 37, line 13 / inserted quantifier and baseline replacement: Restores the explicit quantification over i and records the actual placement of the replacement condition.
- Scan 37, lines 23–24 / final relative verb: The source’s likely d-initial verb remains doubtful; no stronger indexing claim is supplied from H.
- Scan 39, asterisk note / final Sigma index: The marginal local-triviality assertion is written over Sigma with d prime, without editorial repair.
- Scan 40, principal square, extra rightward arrow: Preserves an additional drawn arrow with unwritten target.
- Scan 40, right note, first length condition: Does not conflate the two alternative length assumptions.
- Scan 40, left margin, complete condition a): Restores both indices, the order restriction and the written phi-shaped glyph.
- Scan 40, left margin, parenthetical two-case formula: Preserves the written two-case alternative and equivalence, rather than an unconditional equality.
- Scan 41, line 6, Sigma convention, right-hand sign: Preserves the written phi in the convention; its apparent empty-set meaning is recorded in the notation entry.
- Scan 42, opening / raised Sigma pair: Preserves the written pair of single-index objects rather than replacing the second by an ij-indexed object.
- Scan 42, margin / small intersection formula separators: Retains written grouping/separation within the indices without normalizing the three factors alike.
- Scan 42, weaker condition / headless connector and dotted i: The source is retained as a headless connector; the prose calls the represented map a plongement, but that does not supply an arrowhead.
- Scan 42, couples / negative cofinal condition: H reverses the written selection criterion for the pair of flags by omitting both negation words.
- Scan 42, couples / live de longueur n and placement leader: Retains the manuscript’s simultaneous explicit tuple and live length description rather than deleting the latter.
- Scan 43, first flag chain, continuation and missing inclusion sign: Retains two written continuation marks and does not insert H’s extra subset sign.
- Scan 43, projection chain, first nested index and continuation marks: Keeps p−1 despite the earlier explicit p+1.
- Scan 43, cone tuple and lower identity: Preserves the written p−1 index, one continuation dash, and vertical identity arrangement.
- Scan 43, transition sentence, duplicated marginal words: Preserves the marginal note once and removes its mistaken duplication in the main sentence.
- Scan 43, final map, projection label: Reads pr_2, the projection explicitly labelled but on this leaf.
- Scan 44, top decompositions / corrected summation sign: Preserves an authorial correction in the decomposition rather than presenting only an unqualified coproduct.
- Scan 44, bottom coproduct / lower-limit continuation: The literal written chain has adjacent < signs; no intervening terms or ellipsis are supplied.
- Scan 44, bottom coproduct / Sigma index: Preserves the written single-dash index rather than replacing it by a sequence ellipsis.
- Scan 45, order note, subscript: Preserves the note’s bare z subscript despite the nearby lower-ideal formulas.
- Scan 45, first diagonal note, phantom drapeaux insertion: Removes a duplicate transcription of the next diagonal line’s ending.
- Scan 45, unfinished subset, supplied illegible target: Preserves the visibly unfinished relation without supplying its expected right-hand side.
- Scan 45, lower diagonal marginal note, borrowed main-line words: Leaves the marginal sentence fragment incomplete instead of filling it from crossing main text.
- Scan 46, first main display: headless shaft: Preserves the drawn headless connecting stroke without supplying a map arrow.
- Scan 46, Cela note: d-prime subset d: Restores the written direction of the initial-segment hypothesis.
- Scan 46, star tuple: one dash, one close, no terminal stop: Preserves the source’s written continuation and unbalanced parenthesis rather than completing the tuple prose.
- Scan 48, line 3 / two raised additions and their attachments: The first term is excepted, and the successor condition is expressly in I; H attaches and reads the additions differently.
- Scan 48, line 13 / parenthetical conclusion of condition a: The written conclusion is donc fini; no replacement by a fibres-finiteness formulation is made.
- Scan 48, line 16 / first superscript on Sigma: The first strict-relation object is literally written with <; the later star is preserved separately.
- Scan 48, line 17 / extra linked words after l’ens: The possible connective wording remains unresolved; H’s shorter wording does not exhaust the visible ink.
- Scan 48, line 18 / second superscript on Sigma: The definition of flag spaces uses < locally, despite the later star.
- Scan 48, line 20 / third superscript on Sigma: The local < / < / * variation is retained.

### Unresolved places

- opening diagram / cancelled words: `\unsure{et si|et où}`; alternatives: et si favored by comparison with the live words below; et où remains possible; i.e. is disfavored but the cancellation is not fully recoverable; confidence medium.
- opening diagram / curled mark after E: `\unsure{\}}`; alternatives: closing brace-like mark; function not established; confidence medium.
- below opening diagram: `\unsure{\vert|1}`; alternatives: literal near-vertical pen stroke; numeral 1; incidental pen stroke; confidence low.
- line 5 / first cancelled T cap E: `\unsure{T\cap E}`; alternatives: T and E are recognizable; the intervening cap is occluded by the cancellation; confidence medium.
- line 11 / commutent placement: `\inserted{commutent}`; alternatives: raised insertion after the two lambda expressions; possibly an unusually raised continuation of the same line; confidence medium.
- line 16 / compact punctuation after s'identifie: `\unsure{à|:}`; alternatives: compressed à; colon-like punctuation; there is not a separate à plus colon; confidence medium.

### Unresolved places

- line 1 / overwritten initial strokes of voisinages: `voisinages`; alternatives: Underlying overwritten letters cannot be separated; H’s separate preceding struck word is not established.; confidence medium.
- line 6 / status of b): `\inserted{b) $X=\bigcup_{i\in I}X_i$}`; alternatives: Smaller added line versus ordinary second row; no caret establishes chronology.; confidence medium.
- left margin beside (14) / stroke before union conditions: `\unsure{\{}`; alternatives: Grouping left brace versus overlapping correction/index stroke; no secure cancellation state.; confidence low.
- line 10 / terminal letter at right paper edge: `convenable\unsure{s}`; alternatives: The final s is expected from the visible word but is not independently bounded against the dark physical edge.; confidence medium.
- line 12 / extra ink before E_Balpha: `aux \unsure{l'}$E_{B_\alpha}$`; alternatives: A separate l and apostrophe, or a corrective loop/stroke whose textual role is unresolved; no deletion is evident.; confidence medium.
- M02 / cancelled word after J: `\deleted{\unsure{ensemble}}`; alternatives: The underlying cancelled word remains unidentified; the live ensemble is not used to fill it in.; confidence low.
- line 15 / initial cancellation: `\deleted{\unsure{Soit}}`; alternatives: Soit is retained only as C’s candidate; individual letters remain obscured.; confidence low.
- line 16 / cancellation after et: `\deleted{et \unsure{soit}}`; alternatives: The second word is not securely legible; nearby live soit supplies comparison only.; confidence low.
- line 18 / initial cancellation: `\deleted{\unsure{Alors}}`; alternatives: Alors is retained only as a candidate under dense cancellation.; confidence low.
- line 19 / cluster after équivaut: `\unsure{à|:}`; alternatives: An accented compressed a versus colon; both fit the two remaining blobs.; confidence medium.
- line 21 / des X_i: `des $X_i$`; alternatives: The second C option is favored; the flattened short word remains less distinct than surrounding writing.; confidence medium.
- line 24 / first X and attached foot: `$\unsure{X_i|X}$`; alternatives: Undotted attached i versus a long hooked foot of bare X. There is no independent i-dot here.; confidence medium.

- marginal reference: `\unsure{(cf. p. 15', marge)}`; alternatives: end of marge and closing punctuation crowded; confidence low.
- long marginal intersection, final factor: `\Sigma_{\unsure{i_0i_n}}`; alternatives: i_0i_n; crossing ink prevents full confirmation; confidence low.
- small marginal intersection, first index cluster: `\Sigma_{\unsure{ij},k}`; alternatives: ij overtraced; comma and k independently visible; confidence low.
- small marginal intersection, first right separator: `\Sigma_{i\unsure{,}k}`; alternatives: comma may be present; no separable low stroke is secure; confidence low.
- marginal connector to I: `\Sigma_*\unsure{-|\longrightarrow}I`; alternatives: headless dash; abbreviated arrow; confidence low.
- canceled raised word: `\unsure{deux}`; alternatives: deux; interiors obscured by strike; confidence low.
- canceled condition connective: `\unsure{de|et}`; alternatives: de favored; et remains possible under multiple strokes; confidence low.

### Unresolved places

- crossed predecessor word: `\deleted{\unsure{précédent}}`; alternatives: The deleted interior is obscured; inserted suivant does not establish the old letters.; confidence low.
- crossed prefix before une: `\deleted{des \unsure{filt|fil}}`; alternatives: The final tall stroke may be t or the end of l; no completion to filtrations is supplied.; confidence medium.
- first projection connector: `\Sigma_{d_{p-1}}\unsure{-|\longrightarrow}\Sigma_{d_p}`; alternatives: Long shaft with a tiny terminal blob/return.; confidence medium.
- second projection connector: `\Sigma_{d_p}\unsure{-|\longrightarrow}\mathinner{\cdotp\cdotp}`; alternatives: The right end rises slightly; distinct projecting head not certain.; confidence medium.
- third projection connector: `\mathinner{\cdotp\cdotp}\unsure{-|\longrightarrow}\Sigma_{d_2}`; alternatives: Small thickened termination remains ambiguous.; confidence medium.
- fourth projection connector: `\Sigma_{d_2}\unsure{-|\longrightarrow}\Sigma_{d_1}`; alternatives: More diamond-like terminal blob, still too abbreviated to certify the head.; confidence medium.
- transition ending: `\unsure{transition|transitions}`; alternatives: The final low return does not securely distinguish singular from plural.; confidence medium.
- first marginal heading: `\unsure{NB}`; alternatives: Compressed, joined small strokes; NB remains tentative.; confidence medium.
- section-10 phrase: `\unsure{ab ovo|Ab ovo}`; alternatives: The letter sequence favors ab ovo; initial case remains uncertain.; confidence medium.
- punctuation after fermé: `fermé\unsure{;|,}`; alternatives: The upper point(s) and lower comma-like mark are vertically aligned and may form a semicolon; a comma remains possible. Adding this uncertainty is not counted as a textual correction.; confidence medium.
- last cancelled word: `\deleted{\unsure{plongement}}`; alternatives: The strike obscures the interior; immersion above it does not settle every old letter.; confidence low.

- right insertion / cancelled finis: `\unsure{finis}`; alternatives: Tall initial and dotted strokes survive but the baseline strike obscures individual letters.; confidence low.
- right insertion / terminal curved mark: `\unsure{)|,}`; alternatives: The curve is taller than nearby commas but low near the subscript baseline. A close parenthesis or extended comma is possible; no opening delimiter is supplied.; confidence medium.

- final display / deleted internal close: `\unsure{\}|)}`; alternatives: A curly close or rounded close, crossed by a diagonal; neither is certain.; confidence low.
